# Marginal-gain analysis

All marginal values below are computed only for saved multi-budget runs. Improvement is positive in the favorable direction; lower-is-better losses are sign-adjusted. Native-budget and elapsed-time ratios are task-specific. Rows pair adjacent budgets only within model, seed, split, setting, and source series. Cost per positive improvement is undefined for nonpositive gains. Fifteen later duplicate K-Means records are preserved in raw data but excluded because they reuse the final checkpoint value. ResNet seed-11 time-based marginal calculations subtract the unexplained 1,122.1625-second interval after epoch 2 for the adjusted diagnostic; raw time remains in source records and raw-time figures. The Double DQN CSV omits one success_rate field; derived analysis realigns values, leaves success_rate unavailable, and preserves raw records.

- MLP/LeNet and neural models: epochs; validation accuracy/loss.
- Random Forest: warm-start tree count; validation accuracy/log loss.
- MiniBatch K-Means: partial-fit updates; validation ARI.
- Gaussian Mixture: warm-start EM calls; validation ARI.
- Pseudo-labeling/labeled-only and SSL methods: epochs, paired by seed and label fraction.
- Autoencoder/VAE: reconstruction and linear-probe metrics remain separate.
- RL: environment steps; CartPole and Pendulum remain separate.
- No cross-paradigm pooling. Small-gain screens are descriptive and do not establish saturation.
